# Onsen Gemeral

	id = cosmo_conventaffairs_events.200
	title = cosmo_conventaffairs_events.200.t
	desc = cosmo_conventaffairs_events.200.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {

			}

	}

			}

	}

	}

	}
	picture = {

	}
	picture = {

	}

		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_convent_9	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_CONVENT_EUROPE_9_eventPicture
	}		
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {	
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = convent_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = convent_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = convent_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_conventaffairs_events.200.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			convent_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}

		set_ruler_flag = CONVENT_BRETON_OCCITAIN_8
       }	   	   
		clr_ruler_flag = CONVENT_BRETON_OCCITAIN_a 


		
	}
